         ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
         L1 · System context — the machine manager
         The manager as one double-bordered box that the next section opens; callers, providers, machines, S3 and Slack around it.

         L2 · Containers — request, provisioning and boot
